# Known metadata issues that predate tools/metadata_lint.py.
# `metadata_lint.py check` ignores these lines (each one as many times as it is
# listed) and fails on anything new. Delete a line once its issue is fixed.
#
# Duplicate table names: every NC definition from speeps' set (and lf9veb)
#   gives a few tables the same name although they sit at different addresses.
#   The table tree shows all copies, but name lookups (MCP read/write, compare)
#   reach only the last one. Needs real names per copy.
# LFG1TF000/LFG1TG000 (TCM): 16-bit cells at odd addresses and tables that
#   overlap. Unconfirmed: either sizes/counts are wrong or the code reads them
#   byte by byte. Needs a look at the TCM code that reads them.
LFG1TF000_v02.xml: 'AdaptiveShift_EnableSpeed_6pt' at 0x703EB is not 2-byte aligned but its scaling 'raw_u16' is uint16. The SH-2 CPU can't load a value that size there in one access, so the size is almost certainly wrong. Check the code that reads it
